This dated report records partial source searches. It does not measure global coverage.

AI tools wrote this report from accepted project records.

The dated tables below keep their own source dates and limits. Their counts are not current research totals.

Current research summary. The live atlas has 540 cases and 552 stops. The frozen release has 676 events. The research archive has 129 inactive scientific records. The atlas and data files keep their prior release.

The search log keeps six source inspections with no assigned block. They add no dated search attempts.

The dated comparison at 120 is complete as of 26 October 2026. It uses 796 diagnostic rows and 60 fixed dimensions. The report gives all eleven planned scenarios.

Global median and era median each retain nine factors. KNN unknown and structural zero each retain eight. With global median, all nine factors have close matches. Era median changes one match. At a fixed nine factors, KNN has two fair matches. Structural zero changes four.

Grouped-program median retains nine factors. Linked-program total-one retains nine. The illustrative Shapur three-region scenario also retains nine. Four influence-weight scenarios retain nine. They cover Pometia duplicate annalistic dates and the Velitrae sequence. They also cover Veii before 338 and the Marian Africa program.

Matched results compare 90 with 120. Global and era medians stay at nine factors. KNN stays at eight. Structural zero changes from nine to eight. These trends do not isolate effects of the latest thirty histories.

No scenario retains a tenth factor. The proposed direction stays unknown. It concerns flight or refuge, absorption or coalescence, and low-state cases. Factor order does not identify it.

The report has known values for 788 of 7,200 added cells. Six added profiles have no known values. The supplied analytical era buckets include 99 unknowns. Fixed groups and weights do not prove 120 independent histories. They do not prove additive harm.

Global historical-unit uniqueness and nonoverlap remain UNVERIFIED_UNMET. We cannot claim that all historical units are distinct and free of overlap. Blindness of all source reviews to nomination reasons remains UNVERIFIED_UNMET. We cannot claim that all source reviewers were blind to those reasons. The numerical report is complete. Both global integrity requirements remain unmet.

The next scheduled comparison is 150. The frozen release and active scores do not change. All six whole statistical follow-ups remain open. The older report limits below keep their own dates.

Dated search yields and overlap

These rows retain search yields and overlap from the prior public report. The source dates appear below.

Each yield counts references within a search. References are not unique histories. A null value means unknown or not assessed.

A stored zero does not prove a complete search.
